# Primary sources and exact artifact/access registry

Collector assembly of the two completed waves. No new network retrieval or model execution. A source ID identifies one literal URL; repeated reads, mirrors and copied releases are **not independent confirmations**. Source observations preserve the acquisition layer: landing page, HEAD/range, full bytes/hash, static parse, decode and sampled/full visual review. An HTTP200 HTML page is not a binary. `local_test` in a branch often means parsing or decoding, never implied inference. All research accesses were on 2026-10-23; local assembly date may cross midnight. Per-request timestamps in linked saved JSON logs take precedence over an approximate branch window.

<a id="S001"></a>
## S001 — local original LinQ case, 20 pages

Primary supplied/local document, publisher ООО «ХайТэк» / case organizers as identified in the document. Public URL unknown. Portable byte-identical [PDF](<_WORK/CASE/КЕЙС_LinQ.pdf>) and [direct-reading notes](_WORK/CASE_NOTES.md); SHA256 `7dbece3cf414e2a09ff03c93e9e1ed87172d7724fb7edd5cd3a3ad565063689e`. Root rendered and visually read all20pages on 2026-10-23 after22:12Moscow, recorded22:15:57+03. This is root primary inspection, not collector independent visual reading. Pages4,5,7–10,12–13,15,18–19; publication date not asserted beyond supplied2026case. Supports [F001](RAW_FACTS.md#F001), [F002](RAW_FACTS.md#F002), [F017](RAW_FACTS.md#F017).

Each record uses the acquisition window above. Fine-grained request timestamps are in the named branch access/fetch logs; an approximate window is not an invented per-request timestamp. URLs for the same repository/release are acquisition locators within one provenance family, not separate independent scientific confirmations. Rich source metadata registers are retained once at the end.

README defines TTC to the observation plane, trains with Driving and KITTI, uses PyTorch1.12/CUDA11.3/RTX3090. Its temporal decoder intends current TTC from past images; this is a real temporal mechanism, unlike pixel-distance alarms. Relative speed/accuracy claims in README are author claims and not imported into industrial performance estimates.

Generic YOLOv8 pose supplies 17 COCO points, not a pretrained fence-action class. Pinned generic weight URL [this locator] HEAD200 **6,832,633 bytes**; not downloaded or executed.

<a id="S170"></a>
### S170 — hazard_detector_eval.py

<https://huggingface.co/datasets/govtech/SynthSite/resolve/2904ec01c3dbf2efba09f2cb1b7bdf17841d4d39/code/hazard_detector_eval.py>

govtech/SynthSite publisher; exact individual authors in observations below. immutable revision 2904ec01c3dbf2efba09f2cb1b7bdf17841d4d39; publication date is separate.

Facts: [F121](RAW_FACTS.md#F121).

- [W1_M07:64](_WORK/W1_M07.md): **[primary_docs]** Downloadable code: [this locator] . Its YOLO route names `yolov8x-worldv2.pt`, text classes include person and hoisted load with crane hook, conf0.30/IoU0.40, ByteTrack, all-frame processing. It uses one-second windows with 12 positive votes and fills internal gaps of ≤2 observations. It estimates scale from median person-box height assuming 1.75m. DINO uses `IDEA-Research/grounding-dino-base` without revision pin, two text queries, ~1fps sampling and clip aggregation (≥2 violating samples OR ≥20%). Its normalized geometry filters include load-size and above-person/proximity tests.

Non-commercial use and attribution must be resolved for intended use; public raw availability alone is insufficient. No weight artifact verified. Suitable research for temporal segmentation or normal seated/tool-handling confusers, not a labeled factory near-miss/fall/phone/attachment safety benchmark. Keep paired views of one ID in one split, and confirm subject/procedure independence before claiming unseen-worker generalization.

### W1_M01 — Primary source register

Provenance: [W1_M01:173](_WORK/W1_M01.md).

## Primary source register

All accessed **2026-10-23**, detailed UTC timestamps in local JSON. Commit dates below are repository HEAD commit timestamps, **not paper publication dates**. Sources retrieved on main/master were separately pinned via GitHub API shortly afterward; source/config pinning avoids relying on mutable future docs. Local copies retain retrieval content even where web rendering failed.

* **M01-S001 — MMPose Contributors, “OpenMMLab Pose Estimation Toolbox and Benchmark” (2020 project citation).** https://github.com/open-mmlab/mmpose ; revision **759b39c13fea6ba094afc1fa932f51dc1b11cbf9**, commit2025-08-04T07:30:49Z. License https://github.com/open-mmlab/mmpose/blob/759b39c13fea6ba094afc1fa932f51dc1b11cbf9/LICENSE ; exceptions https://github.com/open-mmlab/mmpose/blob/759b39c13fea6ba094afc1fa932f51dc1b11cbf9/LICENSES.md . Locators: migration section, model zoo, code LICENSE/EDPose row. Local `mmpose_license.txt`, `mmpose_licenses.txt`.
* **M01-S002 — Tao Jiang, Peng Lu, Li Zhang, Ningsheng Ma, Rui Han, Chengqi Lyu, Yining Li, Kai Chen, “RTMPose: Real-Time Multi-Person Pose Estimation based on MMPose” (2023).** https://arxiv.org/abs/2303.07399 ; inspected original project README https://github.com/open-mmlab/mmpose/blob/759b39c13fea6ba094afc1fa932f51dc1b11cbf9/projects/rtmpose/README.md . Locators: Pipeline Performance notes, Model Zoo notes, 17 Keypoints/AIC+COCO/Body8 rows, export/ONNX Runtime examples. Local `rtmpose.txt`, `rtmpose_cfg.txt`, `rtmpose_onnx_demo.txt`, `rtmpose_simcc.txt`. Paper citation is identified from original README; conclusions here rely on inspected source/config, not uninspected paper text.
* **M01-S003 — Peng Lu, Tao Jiang, Yining Li, Xiangtai Li, Kai Chen, Wenming Yang, “RTMO: Towards High-Performance One-Stage Real-Time Multi-Person Pose Estimation” (2023; models released2023-12-13 in README).** https://arxiv.org/abs/2312.07526 ; inspected https://github.com/open-mmlab/mmpose/blob/759b39c13fea6ba094afc1fa932f51dc1b11cbf9/projects/rtmo/README.md . Locators: model zoo COCO/CrowdPose, latency footnote, body7 footnote, config and head links. Local `rtmo.txt`, `rtmo_cfg.txt`, `rtmo_head.txt`.
* **M01-S004 — Yufei Xu, Jing Zhang, Qiming Zhang, Dacheng Tao, “ViTPose: Simple Vision Transformer Baselines for Human Pose Estimation” (NeurIPS2022); ViTPose++ TPAMI2024.** Original repository https://github.com/ViTAE-Transformer/ViTPose/tree/c050ed29112da7704797cc1a65af0234b525010d ; commit2025-12-25T16:25:15Z. Locators: README single-task COCO, multi-task/OCHuman “groundtruth bounding boxes,” LICENSE, B config `data_cfg`. Local `vitpose.txt`, `vitpose_cfg.txt`, `vitpose_license.txt`. Original share access A10.
* **M01-S005 — Ultralytics, “Pose Estimation”, YOLO11 configuration, licensing.** https://docs.ultralytics.com/tasks/pose/ ; https://www.ultralytics.com/license ; source revision **0bcee3df04c7a441b07c755202c2ea962cb7c387**, commit2026-10-23T19:09:22Z. https://github.com/ultralytics/ultralytics/blob/0bcee3df04c7a441b07c755202c2ea962cb7c387/ultralytics/cfg/models/11/yolo11-pose.yaml ; https://github.com/ultralytics/ultralytics/blob/0bcee3df04c7a441b07c755202c2ea962cb7c387/LICENSE . Locators: yaml `kpt_shape`, pose outputs, code LICENSE; local `ultra_pose.txt`, `ultra_cfg.txt`, `ultra_results.txt`, `ultra_license.txt`, `ultra_license_docs_TEXT.txt`. Current docs have YOLO26 examples; no publication date assigned to mutable page.
* **M01-S006 — Google AI Edge, “Pose landmark detection guide for Python”, last updated2026-08-17UTC.** https://developers.google.com/edge/mediapipe/solutions/vision/pose_landmarker/python ; overview https://developers.google.com/edge/mediapipe/solutions/vision/pose_landmarker . Locators: configurations `num_poses`, model table, detect_async busy behavior, output WorldLandmarks. Wrapper revision **fd288e22e1b9fa5c2639dcd01129c15677630057**, commit2026-10-23T18:26:16Z. Local `mp_python.txt`, `mp_python_TEXT.txt`, `mp_task.txt`. API max count is configurable positive integer/default1; finite maximum not established.
* **M01-S007 — Valentin Bazarevsky, Ivan Grishchenko, Eduard Gabriel Bazavan (Google), “Model Card BlazePose GHUM 3D”, April16,2021.** https://storage.googleapis.com/mediapipe-assets/Model%20Card%20BlazePose%20GHUM%203D.pdf . GET200, 1,398,977 bytes. Locators pp1–5: specifications, Apache2.0, out-of-scope, training factors, evaluation. Local `mp_card.pdf`/`mp_card.txt`. This is the model card linked by current overview; do not erase its date or transfer every limitation to all future MediaPipe models without qualification.
* **M01-S008 — Google MediaPipe, legacy “Pose” documentation.** https://github.com/google-ai-edge/mediapipe/blob/fd288e22e1b9fa5c2639dcd01129c15677630057/docs/solutions/pose.md . Locators ML Pipeline and Pose Estimation Quality (single person2–4m, Yoga/Dance/HIIT,17COCO keypoints). Local `mp_old.txt`. No separate document publication date established.
* **M01-S009 — Google / TensorFlow MoveNet artifacts and original explanations.** Google authors Francois Belletti, Yu-Hui Chen, Ard Oerlemans, Ronny Votel, **“MoveNet.MultiPose Model Card”** https://storage.googleapis.com/movenet/MoveNet.MultiPose%20Model%20Card.pdf (GET200,99,465bytes; PDF publication date not visible). Local `movenet_multi_card.pdf`/`.txt`; all six-page specifications/training/eval tables inspected as extracted text. Google-owned model variation https://www.kaggle.com/models/google/movenet/tensorFlow2/multipose-lightning/1?tfhub-redirect=true (dynamic page GET200 is not itself binary download; model page surfaced Apache2.0 variation metadata in web index, cards/API independently checked). Ronny Votel and Na Li, **“Next-Generation Pose Detection with MoveNet and TensorFlow.js”**, **2021-05-17**, https://blog.tensorflow.org/2021/05/next-generation-pose-detection-with-movenet-and-tensorflowjs.html ; TensorFlow tutorial https://www.tensorflow.org/hub/tutorials/movenet . Wrapper revision **c731b9ebbd6f4c9e8bf99b0df76bbdbf9c25b07f**, commit2026-03-28T14:48:38Z; https://github.com/tensorflow/tfjs-models/tree/c731b9ebbd6f4c9e8bf99b0df76bbdbf9c25b07f/pose-detection/src/movenet . Local `movenet_multi.txt`, `movenet_constants.txt`, `movenet_detector.txt`.
* **M01-S010 — Piotr Dollar and Tsung-Yi Lin / COCO API, `cocoeval.py`, version2.0 header.** https://github.com/cocodataset/cocoapi/blob/8c9bcc3cf640524c4c20a9c40e89cb6a2f2fa0e9/PythonAPI/pycocotools/cocoeval.py ; commit2020-02-20T17:25:28Z. * **M01-S011 — COCO Consortium, “Terms of Use” and “Download” original site source.** https://github.com/cocodataset/cocodataset.github.io/blob/master/dataset/termsofuse.htm ; https://github.com/cocodataset/cocodataset.github.io/blob/master/dataset/download.htm ; public https://cocodataset.org/#termsofuse . **Лицензии раздельно.** MMAction repository code Apache2.0 checked at v1.2.0; exact v0.24.1 weight-specific grant и checkpoint provenance notices не установлены этой веткой. Нельзя автоматически объявить их Apache потому, что код Apache. HMDB original URL сейчас redirects на новый lab homepage200, **не dataset page**; пригодное первичное полное условие коммерческого использования HMDB/его movie footage не получено. Сами HMDB videos не скачаны. KTH первичная страница ограничивает использование non-commercial research: public download не разрешает коммерческое внедрение/публичную демонстрацию автоматически. CAVIAR primary page указывает **CC BY-SA** и attribution CAVIAR/IST200137540; версия CC и отдельный текст условий не уточнены. MEVA data CC BY4.0 явно подтверждено собственным лицензирующим файлом; это не лицензия весов и не gait labels. Для target capture нужны собственное разрешение/политика, не перенос прав public dataset.

## Primary sources and access register

**D15-S01 — CVAT.ai / contributors, “CVAT for image”, XML1.1 format, live develop docs; publication date unstated.** https://docs.cvat.ai/docs/dataset_management/formats/format-cvat/ , Interpolation section, rendered lines545–576; GET200 saved `D15/cvat_format.txt`.
